<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_a_mh.html"><b>√ aṃh</b><br></a> √ aṃh, “be narrow or distressing”<br>Such a root is inferable from the derivatives:<br>aṃhatí RV.<br>aṃhú V.B.<br>áṅghri ? B.+<br>áhi V.+<br>áṃhas V.+<br>aṃhīyas B.S.<br>aghá V.+<br>ahī́ RV.<br>The verb-form anāha RV1. is of wholly doubtful meaning and connection.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_1_ak_s.html"><b>√ 1 akṣ</b><br></a> √ 1 akṣ, “attain”<br>Present<br>I.ákṣat RV1., akṣase E1<br>Passive<br>akṣya-<br>Future<br>Simple Future: akṣiṣyati, akṣyati<br>Periphrastic Future: akṣitā, aṣṭā<br>Aorist<br>RAo: ācikṣat<br>-iṣ-Ao: ā́kṣiṣus RV1<br>Perfect<br>ānakṣa, ākṣāṇá ? RV1<br>Causative<br>akṣaya-<br>Desiderative<br>ācikṣiṣa-<br>Derivatives<br>PPP: aṣṭa; 1Abs: akṣitvā, aṣṭvā<br>Derived Nouns:<br>akṣiṣṭha. JB.<br>A secondary root-form from √ 1 aś, aṃś and occurring in a few isolated forms. Indra and Agni are called akṣiṣṭhau vahiṣṭhau devānām at JB. i. 304.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_2_ak_s.html"><b>√ 2 akṣ</b><br></a> √ 2 akṣ, “mutilate”<br>Present<br>V. -akṣṇoti etc. AV. ŚB<br>Verbal Nouns<br>-aṣṭa V.B.S<br>Only used with prefix nis; does not appear to have anything to do with the preceding root.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_a_gg.html"><b>√ aṅg</b><br></a> √ aṅg, “move”<br>Present<br>I. aṅgatu C1<br>Perfect<br>ānaṅga etc.etc<br>Causative<br>aṅgayati<br>A single occurrence, doubtless artificial, in the Nalodya.<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_aj.html"><b>√ aj</b><br></a> √ aj, “drive”<br>Present<br>I. ájati -te. etc. V.B<br>Passive<br>ajyáte etc. RV<br>Future ajiṣyati, ajitā<br>Aorist<br>-iṣ-Ao: ājiṣus LŚS1<br>Perfect<br>ajiṣus etc<br>Desiderative<br>ajijiṣa-<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: -ajita ? ŚB.; 2Abs: -ajya S.; -áje RV<br>Derivatives<br>ágra? V.+<br>ajaka C.<br>ájman V.<br>-āja B.+<br>áj V.<br>ájana V.+<br>-ájya V.B.<br>ājí V.+<br>-ája RV.<br>ajirá V.+<br>ájra? RV.<br>ajá V.+<br>ájma RV.<br>ajvin S.<br>The form ajus only by very doubtful conjecture to RV. V. 6.10; ājiṣus variant to AV. āviṣus; ajita in -prajita, possible for prājita (or pra-jita, √ 1 ji ?).<br>Meanings<br>ájati ájate v drive.<br>apa drive away.<br>abhi drive near or together, join.<br>ava drive down.<br>ā bring near, fetch; approach.<br>ud Ā drive out, take off.<br>upa drive near or hither.<br>nis drive out.<br>vi drive asunder, rush through (A).<br>sam drive together, unite; overthrow.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_a_jj.html"><b>√ añj</b><br></a> √ añj, “anoint”<br>Present<br>I. añjet S1<br>VII. anákti aṅkté etc. V.+<br>Passive<br>ajyáye etc. V.+<br>Future<br>Simple Future: aṅkṣyati añjiṣyati<br>Periphrastic Future: aṅktā añjitā<br>Aorist<br>√-Ao: ajyāt; āñji<br>RAo: āñjijat<br>-iṣ-Ao: āñjīs TA<br>Perfect<br>ānañja B. anajā anajyāt RV.), ānajé etc. RV<br>Causative<br>añjayati etc. S.+<br>Desiderative<br>añjijiṣa-<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: aktá V.+; 1Abs: aṅktvā S., aktvā́ B. añjitvā; 2Abs: -ájya B.S<br>Derivatives<br>aṅga C.<br>-ajya C.<br>áṅjana V.+<br>aktā́ RV.<br>aṅgya C.<br>-āja E.+<br>áṅjas V.+<br>-akti E.+<br>añjaka C.<br>ā́jya V.+<br>añjí V.B.<br>aktú V.B.<br>Meanings<br>ákti aṅkté v PPP aktá smear, anoint, adorn (Ā refl.); glorify, extol; make clear, manifest. Caus. añjayati smear, anoint<br>anu anoint<br>abhi smear, anoint (Ā refl.), adorn.<br>ā anoint, smooth, embellish, glorify.<br>ni smear, anoint; Ā slip into, hide among (antar).<br>prati anoint, adorn.<br>vi Ā anoint throughout; anoint or adorn one's self with (I or A); make clear, bring to notice, betray. Caus. cause to become clear, reveal. abhivi P. become clear, appear. sam anoint, adorn, embellish, honour; join, unite; lick, devour; Ā feed on (I).<br>Cf. abhivyakta, abhyakta, vyákta.<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_1_as.html"><b>√ 1 as</b><br></a> √ 1 as, “be”<br>Present<br>II. ásti sánti etc. (edhí) V.+ (sva sma smahe asate M., syāmahe C1.)<br>Perfect<br>ā́sa ā́sitha etc. V.+<br>Derivatives<br>ásu V.+ ásta V.+-asti V.+-āsa(itiha-) AV.+ stí V.B.<br>Meanings<br>ásti 1 v be, exist, happen, become; be present or at hand; fall or belong to (G or D), be enough for (G), be able to (D); turn to, serve for (2D). astu or evamastu well, so be it. With na not be, be gone. Pers. or impers. with another fin. verb = I happen to or it happens that I, e.g. asti paśyasi do you happen to see ? asmi vikrīṇe I happen to sell.<br>ati be beyond, surpass (A).<br>api be in or with (L), fall or belong to (L or D); partake of (impers. w. G & L).<br>abhi be above, rule, overcome, conquer. win; *fall to one's (G) share.<br>upa be in or with, partake of (A).<br>pari overtake; pass or spend (time).<br>pra be prominent, excel.<br>prati equal, emulate (A).<br>sam equal, reach; be together with (saha).<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_1__rj.html"><b>√ ṛj, ṛñj, arj</b><br></a> √ ṛj, ṛñj, arj, “direct, stretch, attain”<br>Present<br>I. árjati B.U.C<br>IV. ṛ́jyant, ṛjyate RV. ṛñjase (1s.) ṛñjasāná RV<br>VI. ṛñjáti -áte etc. RV. AA<br>VII. ṛñjate (3p.) RV<br>Passive<br>ṛiyate<br>Future<br>Simple Future: arjiṣyati<br>Periphrastic Future: arjitā<br>Aorist<br>RAo: ārjijat<br>-iṣ-Ao: ārjiṣṭa<br>Perfect<br>ānarjus ānṛjús AV1. ?<br>Causative<br>arjayati -te etc. S.E.+<br>Desiderative<br>arjijiṣa-<br>Verbal Nouns<br>ṛñjáse RV1<br>Derivatives<br>arjaka C.<br>ṛjipyá RV.<br>ṛ́jīyas V.<br>-arjya C.<br>ṛjíśvan RV.<br>ṛjú V.+<br>arjana E.+<br>ṛjīpín RV.<br>ráji RV.<br>arjanīya C.<br>ṛjīṣá RV.<br>rájiṣṭha RV.B.<br>-arjayitṛ C.<br>ṛjūnas RV.<br>rajīyas JB.<br>-ṛṅga RV.<br>rāji AV.+<br>It seems impossible to divide these forms (generally distributed to two different roots) from one another. The root has three well-marked stages of development: ṛj or ṛñj in RV., arj in Brāh., and arjaya in the later language. Compare also √ rāj probably a derivative from it. With it is related, further, the anomalously formed stem irajya (irajyáti -teetc. RV., and irajyú RV.), “direct, rule”.<br>Meanings<br>ṛj : ṛñjáti ṛñjáte ṛ́jyati ṛ́jyate 1 v stretch out (intrans.), strive after, long for.<br>abhi grasp, snatch.<br>ā Ā strive after, long for.<br>ni reach, attain.<br>pra strive towards.<br>sam strive together.<br>Cf. irajy, yáti & ṛñjasāná.<br>árj : árjati 2 v reach, get, obtain. Caus. arjayati, arjayate = S.<br>ati get over, admit or remove.<br>anu &<br>ava dismiss.<br>upa admit; Caus. get, acquire.<br>

(kárati -te etc. V.B.S., kriyāma RV., kriyāsam -āsma V.B.S., kṛdhí etc. V.+, kṛṣvá etc. RV., kránt krāṇá RV.; kránta RV.; akat ŚB.); ákāri V.+<br>Th Ao: ákarat etc. AV.B.S<br>RAo: acīkarat C<br>-s-Ao: akārṣīt etc. B.+, akṛṣi etc. B.C<br>Perfect<br>cakā́ra cakré etc. (cakártha cakṛṣé etc.) V.+ (cakriyās acakrat etc. acakriran RV.)<br>Causative<br>kārayati -te etc. B.+ (kāryate etc. B.; cikārayiṣa- in d.)<br>Intensive<br>karikṛ- carikṛ- V.B<br>Desiderative<br>cíkīrṣati etc. AV.+,-te etc. S.+ (acikīrṣīs ŚB.; cikīrṣayati C.)<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: kṛtá V.+; Inf: kártum AV.+,-tave V.B.,-tavaí B.S.,-tos V.B.S.; 1Abs: kṛtvā́ AV.+,-tvī́ V.B.,-tvā́ya B.; 2Abs: -kṛ́tya V.+;-kā́ram B.+<br>Derivatives<br>kará V.+<br>kártṛ RV.<br>kāruka B.+<br>-kṛtha RV.<br>karaṇá RV.<br>kartṛ́ V.+<br>-kūrmi,-in RV.<br>-kra RV.<br>káraṇa V.+<br>kártra AV.<br>-kṛt V.+<br>krátu V.+<br>karaṇīya B.+<br>kárman V.+<br>-kṛ́tya n. V.+<br>kriyā KŚS.E.+<br>-karaṇi? E.<br>kárvara V.<br>-kṛtya a. V.+<br>cákri RV.<br>káras RV.<br>-kāra V.+<br>kṛtyā́ V.+<br>-karikra B.<br>káriṣṭha RV.<br>kāraka E.+<br>kṛ́ti V.+<br>-cikīrṣ C.<br>-kariṣṇu S.+<br>kārin AV.+<br>kṛ́tu (-tvas ) V.+<br>cikīrṣā E.+<br>karúṇa V.B.<br>kāryá AV.+<br>kṛ́tvya RV.<br>cikīrṣu E.+<br>kártva V.<br>kāraṇa S.+<br>kṛtnú V.<br>kārayitṛ B.E.<br>kartavyà B.+<br>-kāraṇīya C.<br>kṛtríma V.+<br>kārayitavya E.+<br>kāru E.+<br>kṛ́tvan V.<br>cikārayiṣu C.<br>kṛ́tvarī AV.<br>-ciṣkārayiṣu E.<br>The root-form skṛ is found especially after sam and pari; but also the aor. nír askṛta RV., and PPP upaskṛta E.+ In V. occur only saṃskṛta and pariṣkṛta RV.AV., and pariṣkṛṇvánti and-vánt RV. (against sám akṛṇvan etc. RV.). But from B. on, forms with sk after sam are frequent, almost to the exclusion of those with simple k; even such as sam askurvata, saṃ caskāra, saṃ skariṣyanti, saṃskārayām āsa, saṃciṣkārayiṣu. Of the derivatives, there are found with saṃ only: skaraṇa S.+, skartavya C., skartṛ E.+, skāraka E.+, skārya E.+, skṛti B.+; also skriyā with saṃ C. and pari C.; skāra with saṃ S.+, pari E.+, and upa C.; and skara with apa C., upa E.+, pari E., and vi ( ?) E.+; finally, ā́skra RV., which is doubtful.<br>Meanings<br>(skṛ), kṛṇóti, kṛṇuté, karóti, kurute (karati & karti), PPP kṛtá (q.v.) make, do, cause or produce anything in or on one's self or others; absol. act, work; bring or help to (D); get or procure for (G), Ā for one's self; make or begin something (A, esp. kim) with something (I); make something (A) of something (I or Ab); make (into), render 2A or A & Adj or Adv in ī, ū – With the A of an abstrans. kṛ answers to a verb of kindred mng, e.g. w. praveśam = enter, w. maraṇam die, w. rājyam reign; esp. in the periphr. Pf, e.g. vidāṃ cakāra he knew. We often use a more special verb, e.g. perform or offer (yajñam), prepare or till (kṣetram), fix (samayam), wait (kṣaṇam), utter (vācam or śabdam), exert (vīryam), show, betray, feel (kṛpām, bhāvam, sneham), enjoy (sukham), marry (dārān), violate, dishonour (kanyām) etc ; put, set in or on (pāṇau, skandhe), take to (hṛdi); ponder, deliberate (cetasi, manasi, hṛdaye), turn, direct (manas, w. L), call, name (nāmnā) etc. Note also dvidhā kṛ divide into two, bhasmasāt reduce to ashes, tṛṇavat value like straw. For other phrases cf. the corr. Adj or Adv Caus. kārayate cause to act or do (2A or A of th. & I of pers.), have something put in (L), also = S. Des cíkīrṣati, the wish to act or do.<br>Int karikrati (3 pl.) do repeatedly.<br>adhi put before or over, place at the head, appoint to (L); make a thing the chief matter or main point; be entitled to (P w. L, Ā w. A or Inf).<br>anu do afterwards, imitate, equal (A or G).<br>apa put off, remove; injure, offend, do wrong (A, L or G).<br>abhi do in behalf of (A); effect, make.<br>ā bring or call near; bring about, perform, prepare; serve a god (G), sacrifice. Caus. call near; ask for (2A). apā keep off, remove, pay (a debt); reject, desist. udā expel, select. upā bring near, fetch; grant, present; acquire, get; invite, prepare, undertake, begin (r.). nirā separate, select; push, away, keep off, reject, destroy; refuse, contradict. vyā divide, separate from (I), analyse, explain. samā bring together, collect, unite; make ready, prepare. is (=nis) arrange, make ready, prepare.<br>upa bring near, impart, grant, offer, render a service (G or L of pers.); further, support; w. skṛ Ā prepare, cultivate (land), adorn. enchase; think of, care for (A or *G); deform, corrupt. pratyupa return a service. tiras put aside, remove, cover, conceal; cut out, supplant, surpass; contemn, despise, blame, abuse.<br>ni bring down, humiliate. vini wrong, injure, offend.<br>nis bring forth from (Ab); drive away (also w. skṛ), dispel, exclude; accomplish, arrange, settle, prepare, bring together; restore, requite.<br>pari prepare (food); w. skṛ make ready, fit out, adorn. puras put before or at the head, place in front, introduce, appoint to (L); show, betray.<br>pra perform, effect, cause, make, render (2A); take, marry; appoint to (L); undo, destroy, kill; violate, deflower; Ā induce, move, cause to do; direct or turn (manas, buddhim) upon (D L). resolve; gain, acquire, effect; put before, mention. vipra hurt, wrong, injure, disappoint (caus. the same*).<br>prati do against, return, repay, revenge (A of th., G, D, L of pers.); counteract, withstand (P of th. and G of pers.); restore, requite, repay. Des wish to requite or revenge (A of th., A or L of pers.).<br>vi make different, change, alter, divide. detail, specify; deform, spoil, destroy; develop, produce; Ā (P.) become different, alter, become hostile, unkind, or faithless (w. G or L); contend, fight.<br>sam (kṛ & skṛ) put together, join, compose, prepare, arrange; consecrate, invest, initiate, marry; refine, embellish, adorn. abhisam (skṛ) arrange, perform. Ā render (2A); consecrate, dedicate. upasam (skṛ) prepare, adorn. pratisam restore, repair.<br>Cf. adhikṛta, adhikṛtya, upaskṛta, nikṛta, pariṣkṛta, puraskṛta, puraskṛtya, prakṛta, víkṛta, saṃskṛta.<br>

E.+<br>-karitṛ B.<br>-kir C.<br>-kiri B.<br>One or two late forms occur with prefixed s: apaskiramāṇa, praticaskare; others are authorized by the grammarians; in derivatives, viṣkira S.+<br>Meanings<br>(skṛ), kiráti, kiráte, PPP kīrṇa pour out, cast forth, scatter, strew, cover, fill.<br>anu scatter, strew; cover, fill.<br>abhi sprinkle, cover, fill.<br>ava pour down, scatter, spread, spend (semen); cast off, reject; sprinkle, bestrew, cover, fill. samava bestrew, cover entirely.<br>ā scatter or bestow abundantly, cover over, fill. samā scatter over, cover.<br>ud whirl up (dust); excavate, engrave.<br>upa scatter or throw down, besprinkle, bestrew. vini scatter, disperse; throw away, abandon.<br>pari scatter or strew about, surround.<br>pra scatter forth, throw down; spring forth, issue, rise.<br>vi throw asunder or about, cast, hurl, scatter, disperse, dissolve, split, rend.<br>sam pour out, bestow richly, cover, fill; mix together, confound.<br>Cf. ávakīrna, ākīrṇa, utkīrṇa, prakīrṇa, vikīrṇa, viprakīrṇa, vyākīrṇa, saṃkīrṇa.<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_k_r_s.html"><b>√ kṛṣ</b><br></a> √ kṛṣ, “drag, plough”<br>Present<br>I. kárṣate etc. V.+-te etc. B.+<br>VI. kṛṣáti -te etc. V.+<br>Passive<br>kṛṣyáte etc. B.+<br>Future<br>Simple Future: krakṣyánt S., krakṣye B., karkṣyati karṣiṣyánt B<br>Periphrastic Future: karṣṭā, kraṣṭā<br>Aorist<br>RAo: acīkṛṣam RV. acakarṣat<br>-s-Ao: akārkṣīt, akrākṣīt<br>-sa- Ao: akṛkṣat etc. B. (akṛkṣathās ŚB.)<br>Perfect<br>cakarṣa etc. (cakarṣatus) B.+<br>Causative<br>karṣayati etc. E.+<br>Intensive<br>cárkṛsati etc. V.B. carīkṛṣ-<br>Desiderative<br>cikṛkṣa-<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: kṛṣṭá AV.+; Inf: kraṣṭum E.+; 1Abs: kṛṣṭvā́ B.+; 2Abs: -kṛṣya S.+<br>Derivatives<br>karṣa S.+<br>karṣū́ B.+<br>kā́rṣi ,-ṣín B.<br>kṛṣya S.+<br>karṣaka E.+<br>karṣaṇa E.+<br>kā́rṣman RV.<br>kṛṣṭí V.+<br>karṣin ,-ṣí B.+<br>karṣaṇīya C.<br>kṛṣí V.+<br>kraṣṭavya C.<br>-kraṣṭṛ C.<br>It does not seem worth while to divide this root, evidently one, into two nearly related ones, as is often done.<br>Meanings<br>kárṣati kárṣate v (kṛṣáti, kṛṣáte), PPP kṛṣṭá (q.v.) drag, draw; pull, tear, bend (a bow); draw furrows, plough (only kṛ́ṣati); draw to one's self, get possession of, overpower. Caus. karṣayati tug, pull, tear, extract; affict, vex (cf. kṛś Caus. ). I. cárkṛṣati plough.<br>ati draw beyond.<br>anu draw after one's self; attract from the preceding, supply (g.); quote.<br>apa draw away, take off; reject, remove; bend (a bow); detract, debase, dishonour. vyapa draw away, take off, doff; reject, remove, abandon.<br>ava draw or take off, remove, drag down.<br>ā draw on, attract; bend (a bow); drag away; take off, extract, remove from (Ab). apā turn away, avert from (Ab). vyā draw to one's self; draw off, remove, avert. samā draw near; extract from (Ab).<br>ud draw out or up, take off; bend (a bow); raise (fig.); rise, be superior.<br>ni draw down. saṃni P. come near, come in contact with (I).<br>nis draw out, extract; tear in pieces, rend asunder.<br>pari draw about, conduct (army); vex afflict; think over, ponder.<br>pra draw forth, conduct (an army); vex, afflict. vipra draw away, remove. saṃpra the same.<br>vi draw asunder, tear in pieces; draw towards one's self (arrow), bend (bow), turn up (foam), drag about, pull; conduct (army); extract, deprive, withhold. sama draw together, contract; drag away, carry off.<br>Cf. apakṛṣṭa, avakṛṣṭa, utkṛṣṭa, nikṛṣṭa, prakṛṣṭa, vikṛṣṭa, viprakṛṣṭa, saṃnikṛṣṭa.<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_kram.html"><b>√ kram</b><br></a> √ kram, “stride”<br>Present<br>I. krā́matietc. krámateetc. V.+ (kramati etc. RV. ? E.+, krāmate etc. U.E.)<br>IV. krāmyati<br>Passive<br>kramyate etc. S.+<br>Future<br>Simple Future: kraṃsyáti -te AV.B.E.; kramiṣyati -te B.+ (akramiṣyat U.)<br>Periphrastic Future: kramitā, kraṃtā<br>Aorist<br>√-Ao: akran ákramus etc. RV<br>Th Ao: akramat -man AV.B<br>RAo: acikramat<br>-s-Ao: akraṃsta -sata etc. V.B.S. (kraṃsate RV.)<br>-iṣ-Ao: ákramīt etc. V.B.U., krámiṣṭa (3s.) RV. (akramīm RV., akrāmīt ŚB.)<br>Perfect<br>cakrā́ma cakramús etc., cakramé etc. V.+ (cákramanta RV.)<br>Causative<br>kramayati etc. B.+; krāmayati etc. B.+ (krāmyate C.)<br>Desiderative<br>cikramiṣati etc. B.U. cikraṃsa-<br>Intensive<br>caṅkram- V.+, caṅkramyáte B.+<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: krāṃtá AV.+; Inf: krámitum B.+,-tos B., krāṃtum E.+; 1Abs: kramitvā E.+, krāṃtvā́ B.+ kraṃtvā; 2Abs: -krámya V.+;-kráme RV.;-krā́mam AV.+<br>Deriv.:<br>kráma V.+<br>-kramaṇīya E.+<br>-krāmya C.<br>-krāṃtṛ E.<br>-kramin E.+<br>-kramitavya E.<br>-krāmaṇa C.<br>caṅkrama AV.+<br>kramya C.<br>-krāma B.<br>-krā́muka B.<br>caṅkramaṇa S.+<br>krámaṇa V.+<br>-krāmin AV.B.<br>-krāṃti B.+<br>-krāmayitavya C.<br>The aor. akran is by some referred to √ krand .<br>Meanings<br>krā́mati krámate v (kramati, krāmate), PPP krāntá (mostly – ṃ & w. P mg). stride, step, walk, go or come to (A or L); go over, pass, ascend, approach (sex.); surpass, pervade, fill, accomplish; Ā proceed well, succeed. Caus. kramayati (krāmayati) cause to stride. I. caṅkramate, caṅkramyáte & caṅkramīti step to and fro, wander, walk,<br>pass.<br>ati step beyond, cross, pass, excel, overcome, pass by, neglect, transgress (abs. or w. A); part or escape from, lose, be deprived of (Ab). Caus. let pass, take no notice of (A). abhyati step beyond or across, pass, excel, overcome, overstep, neglect, transgress. vyati & samati the same,<br>adhi ascend, mount up to (A)<br>anu go on or along, follow; go through, enumerate.<br>apa go or pass away; withdraw or fly from (Ab).<br>abhi step near, approach, attack, overwhelm; enter, begin, undertake. samabhi come near, approach.<br>ava go away, fly; tread upon, overwhelm (A).<br>ā approach, visit, enter, tread upon (A or L); cling to, grasp, attack, overpower, take hold or possession of (A); undertake, begin (Inf); ascend, mount (mostly Ā). adhyā attack, seize, occupy. samā the same + tread upon, overpower, take hold or possession of (A).<br>ud go up or out, depart; escape, fly or withdraw from (Ab or A); pass by, neglect. vyud go asunder, part, go away; overstep, transgress, disregard.<br>upa come near, approach, enter (A or L); go against, attack; treat, deal with (A); (mostly Ā) undertake, begin, be about to (A, D, or Inf). samupa come near; undertake, begin (Ā w. Inf).<br>ni enter, step, tread down.<br>nis go out of (Ab), depart, fly, escape. Caus. cause to step out, expel, lead or take away. abhinis, upanis, & vinis go out, come forth from (Ab).<br>parā step forward, advance, exert one's self, excel.<br>pari walk about (esp. on the stage), circumambulate, walk through, visit; overtake, overcome.<br>pra step forth, set out, go to (A), go across, pass; Ā deal with (L); Ā (P) proceed to, undertake, begin (A, artham, or Inf). saṃpra Ā proceed to, begin (A or Inf).<br>prati & anuprati come back.<br>vi go asunder, part, separate; walk through, bestride; move on, advance, proceed, exert one's self; content, fight against (A). anuvi Ā step after. nirvi step out.<br>sam come together, meet, encounter; go to, enter (A), pass from (Ab) to (L), go through or across, come near, walk along. Caus. cause to pass, transport, deliver; lead to (2A), cast upon (A of th. and L of pers.). anusam follow after (A), also = upasam step towards, come or pass to (A).<br>Cf. adhyākrānta, ākrānta, niṣkrānta, vikrānta, saṃkrānta.<br>

Caus. kṣepayati cause to (be) cast, make to descend through (I) into (L).<br>adhi bespatter, insult, abuse.<br>apa cast off, take away, remove.<br>abhi strike at (A).<br>ava throw down, throw into (L) hurl at (D); take off.<br>ā throw at (L or D), put in (L); strike, hit; draw near, attract; take away, withdraw, cast off; insult, revile. vyā stretch out (the hand), open (the mouth); strike, attract (the mind). samā throw together, pile up; shake, hurl; take away, tear off, expel, destroy; insult, revile.<br>ud throw, set or take up, life, raise; extract from (Ab); cast off, get rid of (A). samud throw up, lift; loosen, deliver of (Ab); annihilate, destroy.<br>upa throw at, hurl against (L); strike, hit, esp. w. words, i.e. either insult, mock, revile, or intimate, insinuate, allege, allude to (A).<br>ni throw down, place, put, apply; deposit, grant, deliver, entrust, appoint (2A or A of pers. & L of th.); leave, abandon, give up. upani put down. vini throw or put down, commit, entrust.<br>pari throw beyond, put or lay round, surround, encircle, place or put in (L).<br>pra throw far, set before; fling at, put in (L); let down.<br>prati hurt, harm, reject, despise.<br>vi throw asunder, scatter, dispel, distribute; extend, stretch out; let loose (the bow-string), shoot off (the bow); *handle, manage. pravi toss, harass.<br>sam throw together, heap up; compress, contract, abbreviate, diminish.<br>Cf. ākṣipta, vikṣipta, saṃkṣipta.<br>

P & pass. mg.) gatá (q.v.) go, move; go or come to, get at, fall into or upon, undergo, incur, reach, acquire (A ~ prati, L, or D); approach sex. (A); move on, wander; keep on (with a PPP); approach mentally, perceive (~ manasā), guess, understand (P. be understood or meant); go away, pass, set out, depart, die. – With the A of an abstrans. often = become or be w. Adj or PPP, as harṣaṃ gacchati he becomes glad, upālambhanaṃ gacchati he is censured (cf. i2). Caus. gamáyati, te cause to go or come, lead or bring towards (A, D, or L); put a person into a condition (2A); grant, impart (G of pers. & A of th.); cause to go away i.e. send forth; pass, spend; overcome; *cause a person (A) through some other (I) to go; make understood, explain; convey the idea of (A), mean, denote. I. approach repeatedly (A). adhyā meet with, encounter, find. anvā go after or along. abhyā come near, approach, visit; fall into, incur. upā & samupā the same. nyā come down to (A). paryā go or come round. pratyā come back, return from (Ab) to (A); return to life, revive, recover. samā come together, associate, meet, encounter (I ~ saha or sārdham); come near, approach, come to (A or L), come back; meet with, find.<br>ud go up, rise, shoot up, grow; come forth, extend, spread. apod, Des apojjigāṃsati wish to withdraw from, to avoid (Ab). abhyud rise, go out to meet (A), extend, spread; agree, consent to (A). pratyud come forth again; go out to meet or to face (A); set out, depart.<br>upa come near, approach, visit; go against, attack; meet, encounter, approach (sex.), undertake; undergo incur; reach, befall, happen. abhyupa come near, go to, join, get at; reach, obtain; admit, consent to (A). samupa come near, go to, undergo, incur.<br>ni settle down, approach (also sex.), get at, incur, undergo. upani & saṃni meet with, encounter.<br>nis go out, depart, proceed from (Ab), appear; go away, disappear; get rid of (Ab); reach, get, fall into (nidrām fall asleep). vinis go out, depart, set off, cease, vanish; get rid of (Ab).<br>parā go away, depart.<br>pari walk about, circumambulate surround, inclose, pervade, spread.<br>pra set out, start, advance, proceed; go to, get at (A).<br>práti go to meet, come back, return.<br>vi go asunder, go away, pass, cease, vanish. Caus. pass, spend (time).<br>sam (mostly Ā) meet, encounter, join, approach (sex.); harmonize, agree; be fit, answer, correspond. Caus. bring together, join with (I); lead to (A), bestow on (L). abhisam & upasam meet with, come (together) to (A).<br>Cf. anugata, antargata, abhyāgata, ā́gata, udgata, upagata, parigata, vigata, saṃgata.<br>

AB.)<br>Intensive<br>jāgrah- , jarīgṛhya-<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: gṛbhītá V.B., gṛhītá AV.+; Inf: gráhītum B.+,-tavai B.,-tos B.; 1Abs: gṛbhītvā́ AV., gṛhītvā́ AV.+; 2Abs: -gṛ́bhya V.,-gṛ́hya V.+;-grábhe RV., gṛbhé RV.,-graham JB.; gṛhaye K.;-grā́ham B.+<br>Derivatives<br>grábha RV.<br>-grahiṣṇu S.<br>grāhyà V.+<br>gṛ́bhi V.<br>gráha V.+<br>grahītavya B.+<br>grā́hi V.<br>gṛhú RV.<br>-grahin E.<br>grábhītṛ AV.B.<br>grā́huka B.<br>-gṛhīti B.+<br>gárbha V.+<br>grahītṛ AV.+<br>gṛ́bh V.B.<br>jighṛkṣā E.+<br>grábhaṇa V.S.<br>grābhá V.B.S.<br>-gṛh C.<br>jighṛkṣu E.+<br>gráhaṇa AV.+<br>grāha AV.+<br>gṛbhá RV.<br>grahaṇīya E.<br>grāhaka E.+<br>gṛhá V.+<br>-gráhi B.+<br>grāhin E.+<br>gṛ́hya AV.+<br>grāhayitavya C.<br>Compare √ glah. There seems to be no good reason why the root should not be given as gṛbh, gṛh.<br>Meanings<br>grah , gṛbhṇā́ti, gṛbhṇīté, gṛhlā́ti, gṛhlīté v (gṛhlati, gṛbhṇā́ti, gṛbhṇīté, gṛhlā́ti, gṛhlīté gṛhlate), PPP gṛbhītá & gṛhītá (q.v.) grasp, seize, catch; take (in various mgs, e.g.) gather, pluck, collect, take up or draw (a fluid); take away, rob, occupy, buy, chose, marry, esp. w. pāṇiṃ or pāṇau take (by) the hand (A); accept, receive; assume, use, put on or in; undertake, undergo, begin; gain over, win; get, acquire, keep, hold; take into the mouth, mention, name; take in with the mind, perceive, observe, understand, learn; admit, approve, obey, follow; take for, consider as (2A); ger. gṛhya having taken i.e. accompanied by, furnished with, with (A); P. gṛhyate be meant by (I). Caus. grāháyati cause to grasp, seize, take etc. (pāṇiṃ the hand, see above); cause to choose or marry (2A), cause a person (A) to be occupied with (I), make learn, teach (2A). Des jighṛkṣati, te be about to seize or take, strive to perceive or understand.<br>anu follow in taking etc., accept, admit, approve, support, uphold; favour (āsanam the seat = deign to sit down), oblige, make happy, esp. anugṛhīto ’smi I am happy or satisfied = thanks for (I).<br>apa take away, tear off.<br>abhi seize, catch, take hold of, take up, put together, put on, set (a blossom or fruit), accept, receive.<br>ava let loose; stop, keep from (Ab); separate, divide (G); perceive, notice, feel.<br>ā grasp, seize, draw tight (the reins); take in, learn.<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_car.html"><b>√ car</b><br></a> √ car, “move”<br>Present<br>I. cárati -te etc. V.+<br>Passive<br>caryáte etc. B.+<br>Future<br>Simple Future: cariṣyati etc. B.+,-te B. RV.<br>-cāruka B.<br>Compare √ cal.<br>Meanings<br>cárati (cárate) v PPP caritá (q.v.) & cīrṇa move, go, drive (w. I), walk, roam, wander through or along (A), pervade, explore (only PPP carita); behave, conduct one's self, act or deal with (I or L), be engaged or busy with (I), have sexual intercourse with (I); continue doing or being (PPP, Adj, ger., or Adv); exercise, perform, produce, cause, effect, do, make, render (2A); consume, eat, feed, pasture. – Caus. cāráyati cause to move, walk, roam, eat, pasture, approach sexually, set in motion, impel; cause to do (2A). Des cicariṣati or cicarṣati try to go, be willing to approach sex. (I). Int carcarīti or carcūryáte (w. P & Ps mg) move quickly or repeatedly, wander, roam (A or L).<br>áccha move towards (A).<br>ati escape, go past (A), transgress, also = seq. vyati commit offence against (A).<br>adhi move on or over.<br>anu move along or through, follow; move towards, strive after, endeavour to, keep or take to, behave, conduct one's self. Caus. cause to be pervaded or explored by (I).<br>antar move between or within, be inside (A, L, or G).<br>apa depart, also = seq.<br>abhi act wrongly towards (A). vyabhi the same; Ā bewitch, practise sorcery.<br>ava descend.<br>ā come near, approach, enter, visit; use, employ, treat (like – vat), apply to (A); behave, conduct one's self; deal with, have intercourse with (I ~ saha), undertake, practise, perform, accomplish, effect, do, make. samā proceed, deal or converse with (I), perform etc. = prec.<br>ud rise (l. & f.); emit, esp. the excrement, also = Caus. uccārayati utter, pronounce. abhyud rise over (A). prod cause to sound. vyud go forth in different directions; be unfaithful to (A), commit adultery with (I); perform, accomplish.<br>upa come near, approach, wait on (A), attend, treat with (I), undertake, begin.<br>nis & vinis go or come forth.<br>parā go away, depart.<br>pari walk about, circumambulate; wait upon, serve. Caus. be attended by (I).<br>pra come forth, appear, succeed, thrive; go towards, arrive at, reach, visit; proceed, undertake, begin; be active or busy with (loc); perform, do. saṃpra begin to move, go on, take place, happen.<br>vi go asunder, open, spread, be diffused; fall on, attack; walk about, roam, wander, go through, pervade; have intercourse with (I); go astray, fail, sin; practise, fulfil, do. Caus. cause to roam or go astray, corrupt, seduce; move to and fro (in the mind) i.e. deliberate, consider, reflect, ponder, examine, ascertain, state. pravi advance, proceed, go about, roam.<br>sam come together, meet, approach, arrive, appear, reach to (ā); spread through, pervade; stay, remain; exercise, fulfil. Caus. make to meet, bring into contact, set in motion, cause to go. anusam go after or along, spread through, penetrate, also = seq. abhisam go towards, seek for.<br>Cf. ācarita, ācaritavya, vicārita.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_carc.html"><b>√ carc</b><br></a> √ carc, “repeat”<br>Causative<br>carcayati C., carcita E.+<br>Derivatives<br>carcā C. carcaka C.-carci S.<br>carcana C.-carcī C.<br>Probably in reality a denominative of carcā, and this a frequentative formation, perhaps from √ car.<br>Meanings<br>Caus. carcayati, PPP carcita (q.v.) repeat, esp. by inserting iti.<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_caay.html"><b>√ cāy</b><br></a> √ cāy, “note, observe”<br>Present<br>I. cā́yati etc. B., cā́yamāna RV<br>Passive<br>cāyyate TS<br>Future<br>Simple Future: cāyiṣyati -te<br>Periphrastic Future: cāyitā<br>Aorist<br>-iṣ-Ao: acāyiṣam AV. acāyiṣṭa<br>Perfect<br>cikyus AA. cacāya -ye. -cāyāṃ cakrus JB<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: citá B.; 1Abs: cāyitvā́ AV.;-cā́yya V.B.U<br>Derivatives<br>cāyanīya C. cāyitṛ C. cāyú RV.-citi B.<br>A form, but an early specialized one, of √ 2 ci. <p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_2_ci.html"><b>√ 2 ci</b><br></a> √ 2 ci, “note, observe”<br>Present<br>I. cayate etc. V.B<br>III. cikéṣi etc. V.B. (cikīhi AV., ciketu TS., cikéthe RV., cikitām AV., cíkyat V., acikayus B.)<br>V. cinoti cinute E.+<br>Passive<br>cīyate etc. C<br>Future<br>Simple Future: ceṣyati E<br>Aorist<br>√-Ao: ácet ácidhvam RV<br>-s-Ao: aceṣṭa C<br>Perfect<br>cikāya etc. V.+<br>Desiderative<br>cikīṣati -te etc. V.B.S<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: citá V.+; Inf: cetum E.+; 2Abs: -citya E.+<br>Derivatives<br>-caya V.+<br>-cāyin E.+<br>-cetavya E.+<br>-ceya E.<br>-cayana C.<br>-cít RV.<br>cetṛ́ V.B.<br>-cira RV.<br>cāyaka C.<br>citi B.+<br>Compare √ cāy, which appears to be an early specialized form of this root; cay ( BR. 3 ci) is another. The forms and derivatives of √ 2 ci are not everywhere well separable from those of √ 1 ci, and there is reason to suspect the two roots of being ultimately one; words with the prefixes nis and vi, specially, are probably best referred to √ 1 ci, with the meaning “take apart”, and so “resolve, decide”.<br>Meanings<br>cikéti , cinoti, cinute (cayate) observe, perceive, notice, seek for, search.<br>anu remember.<br>apa respect, honour.<br>ni notice, observe.<br>nis & vinis examine, consider, fix upon.<br>pari search, examine, find out, discover. P. get acquainted.<br>vi discern, distinguish, make clear, illustrate, examine, try, look for, strive after. pravi examine, try.<br>sam deliberate, ponder.<br>Cf. ápacita, abhiniścita, nicitá 2, niṃścita, paricita 2, viniścita.<br>

AV<br>Perfect<br>tatā́pa tepé etc. V.+ (tatápate RV.)<br>Causative<br>tāpáyati etc.-te etc. AV.+ (tāpyate B.E.; Periphrastic Future: tāpitā E.)<br>Desiderative<br>titapsa-<br>Intensive<br>tātapyate etc. E.+<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: taptá V.+, tapita C.; Inf: taptum E.+, táptos ŚB.; 1Abs: taptvā B.+; 2Abs: -tápya V.+<br>Derivatives<br>-tap RV.S.<br>tápu RV.<br>tápiṣṭha V.<br>-tāpin B.+<br>tapa V.+<br>tápus V.<br>tapiṣṇu E.<br>-tāpya B.<br>tapya E.<br>tápuṣī̆ V.B.<br>tapīyas C.<br>tāpana E.+<br>tápana V.+<br>tapti C.<br>tapyatú V.B.<br>-tāpitṛ ? E.<br>tapaná ,-nī́ V.B.<br>taptavya E.<br>tāpa AV.+<br>tāpayitṛ C.<br>tápas V.+<br>taptṛ E.<br>tāpaka C.<br>tāpayiṣṇú RV.<br>Meanings<br>tápati v (tápate), PPP taptá (q.v.) be warm or hot, burn (I), shine; castigate one's self, do penance, suffer; heat, burn (trans.), distress, pain, torment. P. tapyáte (also tápyate, ti) has only the intrans. mgs. Caus. tāpayati, te make hot, consume by heat, listress, pain, castigate (one's self).<br>ati make warm or hot, trouble, distress.<br>anu heat, pain; P. suffer pain, repent, long for (A). pratyanu & samnu P. repent.<br>abhi make hot, shine upon, pain, ache; P. suffer pain.<br>ā glow, shine, heat; P. suffer pain, w. tapas castigate one's self. abhyā heat, pain, torment.<br>ud the same.<br>upa make hot, vex, hurt; also = P. suffer, pain, get unwell (P also impers. w. G or A of pers.).<br>nis (niṣ) heat, singe.<br>pari burn all round, set on fire, kindle; also = P. feel pain, suffer, do penance (~ tapas). Caus. pain, afflict.<br>pra P give out heat, burn, shine (l. & f.); shine upon, warm, heat, singe, roast, pain, torment; also = P. feel pain, suffer, castigate one's self. Caus. heat, warm, illumine, kindle; pain, distress.<br>prati give out heat towards, make hot or warm.<br>vi give out strong heat, burn; pervade, penetrate.<br>sam heat, burn, pain, torment (also Caus. ); suffer pain, repent. P. be distressed, grieve, do penance. abhisam vex, distress.<br>Cf. úttapta, upatapta, pratapant, pratapta, saṃtapta.<br>

Caus. make descend, lead or bring (down) from (Ab) to (A or L); remove, take off, avert from (Ab); introduce, establish.<br>ā pass, cross, overcome; enlarge, increase.<br>ud the same + come up from (Ab), esp. out of the water (~ jalāt), escape, be saved from (Ab). Caus. cause to come out, vomit up, take off; lead or fetch over, save from (Ab), deliver, rescue. abhyud cross, pass, get through unto (D). samud = ud.<br>ni overthrow, humiliate.<br>nis come forth from, get out of (Ab); pass, finish, accomplish; overcome, master Caus. save, deliver; overcome, vanquish.<br>pra take to the water, cross over; start on, advance, increase, grow; lead on, further, strengthen, promote, prolong (esp. āyus the life); Ā live on. Caus. extend, lengthen; cheat, deceive; lead astray, seduce to (D or L). vipra Caus. cheat, deceive.<br>vi cross through, traverse; further, promote, prolong; remove from (Ab); destroy, frustrate; grant, bestow, give, offer; perform, cause, effect.<br>sam cross over, pass through (l. & f.), escape, be saved; also = Caus. bring over, save.<br>Cf. avatīrṇa, uttīrṇa, nistīrṇa, vitīrṇa.<br>

N). samā assign, impart, communicate (D or G); teach, designate, name (2A); direct, appoint to (D, Inf, or artham), also = Caus. order, command (absol.).<br>ud stretch out towards (A); indicate, explain, teach, designate as, mean by (2A), predict or prophesy of (A); point out i.e. propose or offer as (2A). samud mention, enumerate; designate, name (2A).<br>upa point to (A), show; indicate, explain, mention, speak of (A); advise, recommend; inform, instruct, teach (2A); command, rule over (A). pratyupa teach in return (2A). samupa point to (A), teach, show (2A).<br>nis point to (A), assign, destine, designate, name, regard as, take for (2A); mention, proclaim, predict; advise, suggest (2A). abhinis name, designate (2A); settle, determine. vinis assign, destine for (L); indicate, proclaim; designate, name (2A); resolve, determine; appoint to, charge with (L).<br>pari indicate, state.<br>pra the same + announce, proclaim, direct, order; assign, grant, impart. saṃpra indicate, designate, state.<br>sam assign, destine for; direct, instruct, order; appoint to, charge with (2A). pratisam give a pers. (A, G, D, or *I) to see (A), show, w. ātmānam one's self, i.e. appear, pretend to be; produce, point out, make known as or prove to be (2A); – PPP darśitam (impers.) appearance has been made by (I).* Des dídṛkṣate (ti) desire or like to see.<br>anu look at (A), behold, see (manasā with the mind); P. Ā be seen, appear; Caus. cause to see, teach, show (2A).<br>abhi behold, see; P. be seen, appear; Caus. cause to see, show, betray.<br>ā Ā appear; Caus. show.<br>ud Caus. PPP uddarśitam = darśitam (v.S.).<br>upa look on, notice, observe; P.Ā be visible, appear; Caus. cause to see, show, explain; exhibit, pretend, feign.<br>ni Caus. cause to see, show, point out, explain, teach, proclaim.<br>pari see = visit; behold, notice, know, understand, find out; P. be visible, appear; Caus. show, explain.<br>pra P. Ā be visible, appear; Caus. make visible, show, point out, make known or clear, explain, teach. saṃpra behold; P. be seen, appear; Caus. cause to see, show, ātmānam pretend to be (Adv in vat), point out etc. = prec.<br>prati behold, notice; P. Ā be seen or visible, appear as (N); Caus. cause to see, show.<br>vi P. Ā be clearly visible, come forth, appear; Caus. = prec.<br>sam P Ā behold, observe; P. Ā be seen or appear (together), resemble; Caus. cause to see, make manifest, show; w. ātmānam etc. = saṃpra Caus.<br>Cf. abhyuddṛṣṭa, uddṛṣṭa, saṃdṛṣṭa.<br>

Caus. dhāpayati (only – ṃ). Des dídhiṣati, the wish to give; Ā wish to win or get, w. avadyam bid defiance (G); dhítsati wish to put or place upon (L).<br>adhi P set upon (esp. the fire); bestow on (D or L), grant, impart; Ā acquire, get, exhibit.<br>anu put on additionally; induce to (D). vyanu unfold, exhibit.<br>antar PĀ put into (L) or between, separate, exclude; hide, conceal, cover; P receive or contain within; keep from (Ab), withhold; P. be covered or hidden, disappear.<br>apa P take or drive away.<br>api or pi put in (L); offer or present to (D), bestow on (L); cover, shut up, hide, conceal; P. be hidden, become invisible, disappear in (L).<br>abhi put on or to, surround or cover with (I), give up to (D), deliver; practice, apply, commit; name, designate; tell (2A); P. be called. pratyabhi draw back, reabsorb; answer, consent.<br>ava put down or in, immerse (in water), shut up or enclose in (L); be immersed in (L), be attentive. Caus. cause to be laid in. vyava put asunder, place between, separate, interrupt; P. be separated or interrupted.<br>ā put on (esp. wood on the fire), lay, set, place in or on, direct towards, fix upon (L or D), lay down, deposit; impart, grant, bestow, offer (D, L, or G of pers.), bring to (D), employ at (L); produce, cause, effect; Ā seize, take, receive, conceive (gárbham), appropriate to one's self. atyā place above, extol. adhyā place upon. anvā put on (esp. wood), i.e. set up or kindle (the fire). abhyā the same. upā put on, place upon (L); Ā seize, take. nirā take out or away. paryā lay about, surround. vyā P. be divided, be separated from (Ab); be uncomfortable or ill. samā put on (wood), set up or kindle (fire); lay, set, place on or in (L), turn or fix (the eyes or mind) upon (L), collect or concentrate the mind (ātmānam); impose, adjust, apply; unite, keep together, reconcile, restore, settle, arrange, cause, produce. Ā put on (a garment), conceive (of women), accept, appropriate to one's self, manifest, betray, devote one's self entirely to (A); establish, state, concede, agree. upasamā = anvā + put a thing in its place. pratisamā adjust (the arrow), put in order, restore. udā shut up, erect; cast away, expose.<br>upa put on or in, w. adhastāt under; put on the fire, put (the horse) to the yoke, put to mind (hṛdi); lie down upon (A), (g.) precede immediately (only P.); commit to (D), impart, teach; add, join; use, employ. tiras set aside, remove, drive back, overwhelm; hide, conceal. Ā be hidden from (Ab), vanish, disappear.<br>ni lay down, take off, put, set, place in or on (L ~ antár or antar – ṃ), put before one (D), turn (the eyes) to (L), set the mind i.e. resolve upon (D); deposit, keep, guard; appoint, commit, entrust; keep down, suppress, remove, finish. With kriyām take pains with (L); w. bhūmau or avaṭe bury; w. maulau esteem highly; w. manasi or hṛdaye reflect, ponder; w. máno mánasi consent (lit. put mind to mind). Caus. cause to deposit or to put in (L). adhini lay upon, bestow, impart. apani put aside, remove, drive away. abhini lay on, impose; approach, touch. upani place near, put before (D); lay down, bury (a treasure); deposit, commit, entrust (L); bring about, effect. praṇi lay down, prostrate (the body); put on or in (L); touch, approach with (I), turn or fix (eyes or mind) on (L); be intent upon or attentive to (A); stretch forth, send out (esp. spics, carān), explore, search; find out to be (2A) pratini put instead, substitute; order, command. vini lay apart, distribute, take off or down; put in, fix on, appoint to (L). saṃni lay down (together) in or at (L); place near or aside; keep, preserve, heap up, collect; Ā appoint, assign; w. dṛṣṭim fix on (L), w. hṛdayam concentrate, collect; Ā P. be near or present. Caus. bring near, fetch.<br>nis find out.<br>pari PĀ lay or set around, put on, don (vāsas etc.); surround, clothe, dress; P conclude the recitation (r.). Caus. cause to put on (2A), surround or clothe with (I). vipari change, esp. clothes (vāsas). puras Ā (P) put before, place in front or at the head, prefer, choose, be intent on or eager for (A); appoint (esp. to priestly functions); commit, entrust to (D or G).<br>pra present, offer; set forward, send out (spies).<br>prati put instead, replace; adjust (the arrow), take aim; put to the lips, set the foot upon (L), stride out; present, offer, restore; Ā commence, begin.<br>vi distribute, divide; impart, procure, grant; appoint, arrange, settle; destine, design; exhibit, show, betray; find out, produce, found, form, cause, effect, make (in various mgs & w. various nouns = √ 1 kṛ q.v.); make, render (2A); lay, place, put, fix in or on (L); set forward or send out (spies). Des (dhitsati, te) wish to impart, fix, settle, find out, procure, make or render (2A); intend or purpose i.g. anuvi Ā appoint or regulate (in due order); cause or effect (after another), bring about, perform; P. be regulated according to, i.e. obey, follow (A or G). prativi arrange, prepare, make ready; counteract, take measures against (L). saṃvi Ā dispose, arrange; settle, direct, order, appoint; hold up, carry on, manage, employ, use; make or render (2A).<br>sam put together, connect, compose, unite; close, collect (lit. & fig.); repair, restore; lay down, put in or on (L, esp. śaram dhanuṣi, also absol. = aim, shoot); impart, grant, bestow (together) on (L); Ā bring together, unite; make peace or agree with (I, r. A); stand out against (A), rival, vie. P. be united with = get possessed of (I); be held or contained in (L). anusam add, connect; follow (lit.* & fig.), observe, explore, ascertain. abhisam snap at (L), unite or join, esp. the arrow with the bow (I); bring about, beget; aim at (D or L), purpose, intend (A or D); call to mind, ponder; consent, agree about (A); win over, take in, cheat, deceive. upasam add, increase; join or connect with (I); aim at, keep in view; cheat, deceive. pratisam put together (again), readjust; Ā put on (esp. the arrow on the bow), restore, return.<br>Cf. atyāhita, antárhita, ápihita, abhíhita, ávahita, ā́hita, úddhita, upasaṃhita, upahita, upādhā́ya, upā́hita, tirohita, níhita, párihita, purohita, praṇihita, vinihita, vihita, vyavahita, vyā́hita, sáhita, saṃnihita, samā́hita.<br>

Caus. cause a person (A) to be received as a pupil. samupa draw near, admit, lead to (A), present, offer; lead away, take to one's self; bring about, cause.<br>ni lead to (D or L), cause to (D Inf); bend, incline; pour out or in; offer, sacrifice; perform, accomplish.<br>nis lead or take away; find out, settle, decide.<br>parā lead away or back.<br>pari lead or carry round, esp. lead a bride round the fire, i.e. marry; lead away, carry off, lead near, bring to (A); find out, explore. Caus. pariṇayayati & pariṇāyayati marry a man (A) with a woman (A).<br>pra bring forwards, lead further, promote; convey (r.); conduct or take to (Ā to one's self), present, offer; bring or reduce to (a state); employ, inflict (punishment); state, decide, teach, compose, promulgate; perform, accomplish; show, betray (esp. one's feelings), love, desire. saṃpra bring together, collect, raise (taxes); employ, inflict (punishment); compose, promulgate.<br>prati lead back or towards, put together, mix.<br>vi lead or drive away, remove; spend, pass (time); mix, stir up (Soma etc.); part (the hair); spread, extend; train, discipline, teach, instruct; cause or induce to (D Inf); perform, accomplish.<br>sam lead together, gather, collect, unite; mix together, stir up (r.); bring near, fetch, get, procure; present with (I); give back, restore, return.<br>Cf. apanīta, abhinīta, abhivinīta, nirṇīta, pariṇīta, práṇīta, vinīta.<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_3_paa.html"><b>√ 3 pā</b><br></a> √ 3 pā, “rise against”<br>BR. refer to such a root certain forms from the reduplicated middle stem pipī with prefix ud. Des bíbhitsati wish to break or destroy.<br>ava cleave asunder, pierce.<br>ud break through (disclose, betray*); P. burst open, become manifest<br>nis split or tear asunder, pierce, hurt, wound, loose, unveil, discover; P. split, burst, open (intrans.).<br>pari P. be split, broken, changed, disfigured.<br>pra split forth, tear asunder, open, pierce, divide.<br>prati pierce, penetrate; disclose, betray; reproach, censure.<br>vi split or break asunder, pierce, destroy, divide, disunite, change, alter. Caus. alienate, estrange from (Ab).<br>sam cleave, split, break, pierce, hurt, wound, interrupt, give up; combine, mingle, join, associate with (A). anusam & upasam bring together, mingle, mix.<br>Cf. udbhinna, nirbhinna, prodbhinna (add.), vibhinna, saṃbhinna.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_bhi_saj.html"><b>√ bhiṣaj</b><br></a> √ bhiṣaj, “heal”<br>Present<br>II. bhiṣakti RV.<br>VII ? abhiṣṇak RV.<br>Derivatives<br>bhiṣáj V.+<br>bheṣajá V.+<br>The use of bhiṣaj as a verbal root in RV. is very abnormal. BR. and Gr. refer, perhaps with reason, the reduplicated present forms to another root, meaning “delay” (√ 2 mad, mand BR.; √ 2 man Gr.).<br>Meanings<br>mányate mányati manuté v PPP matá (q.v.) think, believe, imagine; consider as or take for (2A, A & D, or A & Adv in vat), also refl. consider one's self as, pass for, appear as (N iva); think fit or right, approve of (A); think of, meditate on (as in prayer), intend or wish for, remember, mention, declare (A, r. G); find out, invent; perceive, observe, know, understand, comprehend (G or A). With bahu esteem, honour, w. laghu disesteem, despise, w. sādhu (iti) approve, commend, w. tṛṇāya (cf. above) value at a straw, esteem lightly, w. na think nothing of, disregard; manye methinks-(often inserted parenthetically). Caus. mānayati (te), PPP mānita honour, esteem. Des mīmāṃsate (ti) consider, examine, call in question.<br>ati value lightly, slighten; be proud or conceited.<br>anu assent, approve, like; acknowledge as (2A); grant, impart; allow, permit; indulge, forgive; w. na have no patience with, dislike. Caus. ask (A) for permission, take leave of (A), beg for (A); honour, regard, consider.<br>ava disregard, despise.<br>abhi put one's mind upon, wish for (A), love, like; have (evil) intentions against (A), threaten, menace, hurt, injure, kill; think, suppose, take for or consider as (2A, P. 2 N).<br>ava despise, treat contemptuously, disregard. abhyava the same.<br>pari overlook, neglect, forget.<br>prati answer, return, oppose (2A). Caus. honour, applaud, regard, consider, deign to accept.<br>vi distinguish. Caus. disgrace, disregard, slighten.<br>sam think, suppose, take for (2A); intend, purpose; esteem, honour. Caus. honour, revere.<br>Cf. ánumata, abhimata, vimata, saṃmata.<br>

Caus.<br>prati receive back, recover, get, obtain; get it i.e. be punished; perceive, learn, understand, know as (2A); wait for, expect.<br>vi take asunder or away; get, procure; cede, surrender, yield. Caus. let a pers. have a th. (2A).<br>sam wrestle or struggle with (I), take hold of one another; obtain, receive.<br>

Int vanīvāhyáte carry hither and thither.<br>ati carry over or across, pass by; spend (time). Caus. transport, transpose, let pass i.e. spend (time), escape (a foe etc.).<br>anu carry along.<br>apa carry or drive away (also Caus. ); take off, remove, give up.<br>abhi convey near or towards, bring.<br>ā the same, also bear (fig.), lead home i.e. take to wife; carry or drive away; intrans. flow. Caus. call near, invite (esp. gods to a sacrifice). anvā & abhyā bring hither or to. udā & samudā lead away, esp. lead out a bride from her father's house, i.e. marry. samā bring hither or to (together), assemble; Ā meet.<br>ud & samud lead up or away; raise, elevate; draw out, extract; marry (cf. udā); lead or bring to, offer, present; bear, carry (lit. & fig.); have, possess, enjoy; endure, suffer; manifest, betray.<br>ud Caus. (cause to) marry.<br>upa lead or bring near, get, procure, cause, effect; betray into (A). samupa carry along, let flow; P. approach, begin, rise.<br>ni lead down or towards (D or L); bear, support.<br>nis lead out, extract, save from (Ab); carry off, remove; bring about, effect; succeed (of pers. & th.). Caus. pass, spend (time); bring about, perform, accomplish.<br>parā lead or take to (D).<br>pari lead about, esp. the bride or the wedding procession, i.e. wed; intrans. flow about.<br>pra trans. carry onward or along, lead or bring to (A); intrans. be borne along, flow, blow. Caus. set in motion, send away, dismiss.<br>prati conduct towards or near.<br>vi lead away, marry (cf. udā), form a matrimonial alliance with (I); Ā get married, celebrate a wedding. Caus. give in marriage or marry.<br>sam carry (together), conduct, lead, draw, drag, bear along; P. ride on (I). Caus. bring together, assemble, collect; drive (carriage etc.), guide, conduct, lead, take to wife; carry the hand along the body, stroke.<br>Cf. apoḍha, udūḍha, upoḍha, óḍha, vyūḍha.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_1_vaa.html"><b>√ 1 vā</b><br></a> √ 1 vā, “blow”<br>Present<br>II. vā́ti etc. V.+<br>IV. vā́yati etc. V.+,-te etc. E<br>Future<br>Simple Future: vāsyati etc. C<br>Aorist<br>-siṣ-Ao ? avāsīt B<br>Perfect<br>vavau etc. B.+<br>Causative<br>vāpáyati etc. V.+<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: vāta S., vāna C.; Inf: vātum C<br>Derivatives<br>-vā AV.B.<br>vā́ta V.+<br>-vāpaka C.<br>-vāpayitṛ C.<br>-vāna E.+<br>vāyú V.+<br>-vāpana C.<br>-vāpayitavya C.<br>-vāyyà AV.<br>The forms from pres.-stem vāya have mostly the sense “be blown or ex-hausted”, and are generally referred to a different root, √ 2 vā. Caus. vedhayati pierce, hit, vyādhayati cause to pierce etc.<br>ati & vyati pierce, perforate.<br>anu pierce or wound (after another); intertwine, set or fill with (I).<br>apa hurl or throw away, give up, neglect.<br>abhi wound, hit.<br>ava precipitate into (L) or from (Ab).<br>ā throw, bore or break through; scare, drive away, also = vyā & samā wave, swing, brandish.<br>ni throw down, overthrow; shoot at, hit, pierce.<br>nis wound, kill, slay.<br>parā hurl off wound, hit.<br>pari shoot at.<br>pra hurl, throw, shoot, perforate, wound.<br>prati shoot, hit.<br>vi pierce, shoot.<br>sam shoot.<br>Cf. anuviddha, apaviddha, ā́viddha, práviddha, vyapaviddha.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_vyay.html"><b>√ vyay</b><br></a> √ vyay, “expend”<br>Present<br>I. vyayati -te etc. C<br>Perfect etc.<br>vavyāya vavyaye etc.etc<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: vyayita C<br>Is only a conjugation of vi + √ 1 i, or denomminative of vyaya.<br>Meanings<br>vyayati vyayate v waste, squander, expend.<br>

RV.<br>-śíkman MS.<br>śáciṣṭha V.B.S.<br>śikṣeṇya S.<br>śácī V.+<br>śagmá V.B.S.<br>śikṣā V.+<br>śikṣú RV.<br>śāci ? RV.<br>śakrá V.+<br>śīkṣā B.+<br>śikṣuka GB.<br>The desiderative śikṣ has won a so independent use and value that it might well enough have been treated (like bhakṣ etc.) as a separate root. BR. devide śak (and śikṣ) into two roots, the second meaning “help”, but favor their ultimate identity.<br>There are other groups of words, not connectable with this or any other verbal root: thus, śakuná, śakúni, śakúnta, śakúnti V.+; śáka, śakán, śákṛt V.+; śaṅkú V.+, śakti (”spear”) E.+<br>Meanings<br>śaknoti v PPP śakta (q.v.) & śakita be able, P. śakyate impers. it is possible to (Inf) or pers. he is able to be, he can or may be (Inf w. pass. mg); be serviceable or helpful to (D), make another partake of (G). Des. śikṣati (q.v.).<br>anu be equal in (A).<br>ā assist (D), help a pers. (D) to (G), invite to (A).<br>upa help, impart.<br>pari master, overpower.<br>pra be able to (Inf).<br>sam be able to (Inf), w. na not be equal to or a match for (L or I).<br>

P trans. lean, lay against or on, turn or transfer to, spread (esp. light) over (L); ĀP.(P) intrans. lean or rest upon, lie or be situated in or on, join one's self with (A or L); Ā(P) resort to, strive after (A); get entrance, befall (A or L); incur, undergo, get into a state or condition.<br>adhi P spread over (A or L); put on (L, esp. on the fire agnau); lie or sit down upon (A). pratyadhi P place near (the fire). samadhi P (agnau) place over the fire.<br>apa Ā lean, rest.<br>abhi bring near; resort to (A).<br>ā P fix on (L); touch, befall (A). Caus. ucchrāpayati lift, raise, increase, enhance. abhyud Ā rise, get up. pratyud rise against (A). samud lift, raise.<br>upa P (trans.) lean against (L); Ā lean or rest upon (A); PĀ join, meet (A).<br>pari lay about; enclose, fence, surround.<br>pra lean forward or against; join with, add to (L).<br>vi P put asunder, open, expand; Ā refl.<br>sam P put together, mix, mingle, join (Ā refl.); Ā(P) betake one's self to, seek protection from, depend on, take to, partake of. abhisam seek help from or in; take to, partake of, use, employ (A). upasam go to meet, join, attend, depend on (A).<br>Cf. ádhiśita, abhyúcchrita, āśita, úcchrita, praśita, procchrita (add.), sā́śata, samāśita, samucchrita.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_zri_s.html"><b>√ śriṣ</b><br></a> √ śriṣ<br>see √ śliṣ.<br>
<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_1_zrii.html"><b>√ 1 śrī, śṛ</b><br></a> √ 1 śrī, śṛ, “mix”<br>Present<br>IX. śrīṇā́ti śrīṇīté etc. V.B<br>Aorist<br>RAo: aśiśrayus RV<br>Perfect<br>śiśriye V<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: śrītá RV., śīrta RV., 1Abs: śrītvā S<br>Derivatives<br>-śir V.B.S.<br>-śira S.+<br>-śrī V.<br>śrayaṇa S.<br>Is probably only a form of √ śri, from which it is at some points hardly separable. The interrelations of the roots śṛ, śrā, śri, śrī are intricate, and not yet fully worked out. Especially doubtful is it what is the connection with them of the noun śrī́ V.+ “fortune”, and the related śriyás RV., śréyas V.+, śréṣṭha V.+, śremán B.<br>

ĀP. be heard of, be known as or to be (2 N); be studied, taught, or read. Caus. śraváyati & śāváyati (te) cause a person to hear, i.e. address, inform of, communicate to, tell (A of thing & A, G, or D of pers.); let hear i.e. proclaim, announce, recite. <p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_2_sidh.html"><b>√ 2 sidh</b><br></a> √ 2 sidh, “succeed”<br>Present<br>IV. sídhyati etc. RV1.U.S.+,-te etc. E.+<br>Future<br>Simple Future: setsyati -te etc. E.+<br>Periphrastic Future: seddhā<br>Aorist<br>Th Ao: asidhat<br>RAo: asīṣidhat<br>-s-Ao: saitsīt C1<br>Precative: sidhyāsam<br>Ao/Ps/3/sg: asedhi<br>Perfect<br>siṣedha RV1. ?<br>Causative<br>sedhaya-<br>Desiderative<br>siṣitsa-<br>Intensive<br>seṣidh-<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: siddha B1.E.+ 1Abs: sedhitvā, sidhitvā, siddhvā<br>Derivatives<br>-sidh RV.<br>siddhi U.+<br>sidhmá RV.<br>-sídhvan RV.<br>sidhrá RV.<br>Appears to be a weakened form of √ sādh. PB<br>Aorist<br>√-Ao: ásṛgran -ram RV.; ásarji V.B.S.; aṛjāná RV<br>RAo: asīsṛjat, asasarjat<br>-s-Ao: asrākṣīt etc. V.+ (asrāk V.B.C1., asrāṭ B., srās AV.; srakṣat B.), ásṛkṣi ásṛṣṭa etc. V.B<br>Perfect<br>sasarja sasṛré etc. V.+ (sasarktha C. sasarjitha sasraṣṭha sasṛjmáhe -jrire RV., sasṛgmáhe SV.; sasṛjyāt asasṛgram RV.)<br>Causative<br>sarjayati -te etc. B.+<br>Desiderative<br>sisṛkṣati etc. B.+ (sisṛkṣmas C1.),-te GB<br>Intensive<br>sarīsṛj-<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: sṛṣṭá V.+; Inf: sraṣṭum E.+; 1Abs: sṛṣṭvā B.U.; 2Abs: -sṛ́jya B.+;-sárgam B.,-sárjam B<br>Derivatives<br>-sṛj V.+<br>-sargin S.+<br>sṛ́ṣṭi V.+<br>sṛjayá -yā́ B.<br>sráj V.+<br>sarjá B.+<br>sṛṣṭí ŚB1.<br>sisṛkṣā C.<br>sṛ́jya B.+<br>-sarjya E.<br>sraṣṭavya C.<br>sisṛkṣu E.+<br>sárga V.+<br>sárjana V.+<br>sraṣṭṛ V.+<br>sarjayitavya U.+<br>Meanings<br>sṛjáti sṛjáte sárjati v PPP sṛṣṭá 1 (q.v.) let loose, hurl, throw, pour out, emit, send forth; create, produce, beget, make; procure, bestow; use, employ. Caus. sarjayati, te. Des sisṛkṣati, te wish to let loose, Ā (P) wish to create.<br>ati hurl off, let loose, give up, dismiss, give leave (A); deliver, free from (2A); yield, grant, bestow; Ā create beyond or as something higher.<br>anu let loose, dismiss, pour out; deliver, grant, bestow; create after (A).<br>api add to, mingle with (L).<br>abhi hurl upon (A), let loose, give free, deliver, abandon, promise, grant, bestow.<br>ava shoot off, pour out, let flow, give up (prāṇān), deliver over, grant, bestow; send away, dismiss (Ā abdicate); remit, pardon; place in, fix on (L). abhyava dismiss, send forth to (A), let loose, let go (the reins). upāva shoot off, admit (esp. the calf to the cow), give over to (A). vyava hurl upon (G), set down, send away, dismiss; hang or fix on (L). samava hurl upon (L), let loose, let go, abandon, omit.<br>ā shoot hither, pour in or out, admit (esp. to copulation). samā hang, fasten to (L), entrust to (L).<br>ud cast, hurl, pour out, shed, take off, lay down, depose, place in (L); stretch out, expand, open, set free, send forth, dismiss, give up; relinquish, abandon, despise, repudiate, renounce, resign; omit, pass over; entrust, commit; produce, create. samud hurl, send forth, utter, throw down, cast away, take off; dismiss, set free, let go, relinquish, abandon, expose; deliver up, hand over.<br>upa hurl, cast, let flow, pour upon; send or bring to (A or D); admit, add, augment; affect or visit with (I), vex, plague.<br>nis pour out, let flow, set free, dismiss, deliver from (Ab), separate, remove.<br>pra let run or flow, open, set free, indulge, give up, resign, quit, abandon.<br>prati hurl against (lit. & fig.), send back.<br>vi throw, shoot, hurl (lit. & fig.), pour out, send forth, dismiss, emit, utter; let loose, set free, deliver from (Ab); cast away, relinquish, abandon; despise, repudiate, renounce, resign, Ā cease from (Ab); yield, grant, bestow; create, produce. Caus. hurl, send forth, emit, utter, set free, leave alone, spare avoid, shun; abandon, expose; yield, grant, bestow, hand over (L); create, produce. anuvi, abhivi & prativi shoot at (A).<br>sam shoot or throw with (I); bring together, mix, unite (Ā P. refl.); endow or furnish with (I); create.<br>Cf. upasṛṣṭa, nisṛṣṭa, sáṃsṛṣṭa.<br>

Caus. sarpayati & Des sísṛpsati only – ṃ; Int sarīsṛpyate creep to and fro.<br>ati glide along or over (A).<br>adhi & samadhi glide over (A).<br>anu glide after or along, approach (A). samanu approach.<br>apa & vyapa move off, recede.<br>abhi steal upon (A).<br>ava glide down (of the setting sun); steal upon, cover gradually (A). anvava glide after. upāva, paryāva & pratyava approach gently. vyava creep into (A).<br>ā creep near. upodā creep forth.<br>ud creep out or up, rise (lit. & fig.); move gently on. Des wish to rise. upod & vyud creep forth. samud arise, appear, begin.<br>upa go gently to, steal upon, creep or fall into (A); move on, come near, approach (also sexually). samupa approach.<br>nis & vinis creep out.<br>pari go round, roam through.<br>pra creep near or into, steal upon (A), move on, break forth, spread, proceed.<br>prati creep back or into.<br>vi move about or along (A); move asunder, dispense, spread, expand.<br>sam creep along or into (A); move onward, approach. anusam creep after. upasam creep near, steal upon (A).<br>

<p class="left"><span lang=EN-GB> <a target="_blank" href="http://www.warnemyr.com/skrgram/grammar/roots/root_2_haa.html"><b>√ 2 hā</b><br></a> √ 2 hā, “go forth”<br>Present<br>III. jíhīte jíhate etc. V.+ (jihīthām ŚB.)<br>Passive<br>hāyate<br>Future<br>Simple Future: hāsyate etc. B.E<br>Periphrastic Future: hātā<br>Aorist<br>-s-Ao: ahāsta etc. V.+<br>Perfect<br>jahiré AV<br>Causative<br>hāpayati etc. AV.B. ajīhapat<br>Desiderative<br>jihīṣate AV. ?<br>Intensive<br>jāhā-<br>Verbal Nouns<br>PPP: hāna B.; Inf: hā́tum B.E.; 1Abs: hātvā; 2Abs: -hā́ya V.+<br>This is only the middle conjugation of √ 1 hā, with the signification slightly weakened or generalized. Caus. (hāpayati) open (trans.).<br>sam arise, stand up.<br>

G of stake); *manage, use, employ (A). P. be called or named.<br>ā bring near, attract, fetch, get, procure, offer, present, give; receive, accept; take to wife, marry; eat, enjoy; take away, sever, remove, destroy, turn off, withdraw from (Ab); utter show, betray; name, call. Des be willing to procure or acquire. anvā repair, complete, supply. abhyā offer, present. udā bring near, fetch, bring out, say, tell, quote (esp. as an example), recite, enumerate, praise, commend; P. be called, mentioned, or quoted. abhyudā bring hither. prabhyudā answer; quote (as a counter example). samudā say, tell, mention. upā fetch, get, procure, offer, present, take hold of, win, acquire, undertake, accomplish. samupā fetch, procure, offer, present, undertake, commence. upanyā offer, present. paryā hand over; turn, invert. pratyā draw back, withdraw, get back, recover; take up again, carry on, prosecute; utter, tell, report. vyā bring out, utter, speak to or with (A or saha), sing (of birds). anuvyā utter successively or in its turn, (answer by singing*); abuse, revile, curse. abhivyā utter, pronounce, recite; bespeak, charm with words, conjure, curse. Caus. (cause to) utter or pronounce. pravyā utter, speak. samā bring together, gather, collect, assemble, combine, comprehend; draw back, withdraw, bring back, restore; captivate, charm. anusamā arrange or put in order (again).<br>ud take out or from (Ab), stretch out (pāṇim); offer, present; lift up, raise, further, strengthen; set aside, select; choose, prefer; extricate, rescue, save; take away, cut off, sever (head), remove, destroy; except. Caus. (cause to) draw out or rescue. Des wish to save or relieve. abhyud take or draw out (in addition), lay aside; rescue, save, recover; start, further, promote. samabhyud, prod & pratyud draw out, lift up, rescue, relieve, promote, further. samud draw or take out, rescue, deliver from (Ab); deduct; remove, destroy; lift up etc. = prec.<br>upa bring, fetch, proffer, present; Ā accept, receive, gather, collect. Caus. proffer, serve (food).<br>nis take or carry out, extract, export (goods); Ā remove, exclude or deliver from (Ab); get rid of (A); change or mix with (I). vinis draw out, extract, take (vṛddhim interest); remove, destroy.<br>pari carry or move about; surround, gird (Ā refl.); protect from (Ab); keep off, shun, avoid, beware, escape; save, spare, except. vipari transpose, interchange.<br>pra stretch forth; fix in, hurl upon (L); offer, present; strike, hurt; attack, assail (A, L, D, or G); Ā fight, make war. anupra throw into the fire. saṃpra hurl, cast; attack, assail (A or L); Ā (P) engage in battle, fight hand to hand.<br>prati throw, hold, or bring back; offer, present. Ā take, eat.<br>vi take apart, divide, separate; take or carry away; shed (tears); transpose, interchange; roam about, pass one's time (A) pleasantly, enjoy one's self.<br>sam bring or draw together, contract, collect, gather; take away, rob; suppress, absorb, destroy. upasam draw together or in, gather, contract, absorb, suppress. pratisam draw together or back, take off, avert, absorb, suppress, destroy, annihilate.<br>Cf. abhivyā́hṛta (sic!), uddhṛta, vihṛta, vyāhṛta.<br>

Caus. excite, gladden.<br>pari & saṃpari Caus. delight greatly, enrapture.<br>pra be merry, rejoice. saṃpra the same; Caus. gladden.<br>prati be glad in return.<br>sam be excited, rejoice, exult. Caus. gladden, delight.<br>Cf. parihṛṣṭa, prahṛṣṭa, proddhṛṣita (add.), saṃhṛṣṭa.<br>
